def

def
 
receive_message(self,
 
conn):

=
 
self.client.recv(4)

=
 
int.from_bytes(raw_msglen,
 
byteorder='big')
 
    
data
 
=
 
b''
 
    
while
 
len(data)
 
<
 
msglen:
 
        
more
 
=
 
conn.recv(msglen
 
-
 
len(data))
 
        
if
 
not
 
more:
 
            
raise
 
EOFError("Socket
 
closed
 
before
 
receiving
 
all
 
data")
 
        
data
 
+=
 
more
 
    
data
 
=
 
data.decode()
 
    
ciphered
 
=
 
[int(char)
 
for
 
char
 
in
 
data.split(",")
 
if
 
char.isdigit()]
 
    
decrypted_data
 
=
 
decrypt(self.private_key ,
 
ciphered)
 
    
return
 
decrypted_data

def
 
receive_message
(
self
,
 
conn):
 
       
raw_msglen
 
=
 
self
.client.recv(
4
)
 
       
msglen
 
=
 
int
.from_bytes(raw_msglen,
 
byteorder
=
'big'
)
 
       
data
 
=
 
b''
 
       
while
 
len
(data)
 
<
 
msglen:
 
           
more
 
=
 
conn.recv(msglen
 
-
 
len
(data))
 
           
if
 
not
 
more:
 
               
raise
 
EOFError
(
"Socket
 
closed
 
before
 
receiving
 
all
 
data"
)
 
           
data
 
+=
 
more
 
       
data
 
=
 
data.decode()
 
       
ciphered
 
=
 
[
int
(char)
 
for
 
char
 
in
 
data.split(
","
)
 
if
 
char.isdigit()]
 
       
decrypted_data
 
=
 
decrypt(
self
.private_key,
 
ciphered)
 
       
return
 
decrypted_data

.client)

def
 
streamer
(
self
):
 
       
while
 
not
 
self
.stop_streaming.is_set():
 
           
try
:
 
               
employees
 
=
 
self
.db.get_all_employees()
 
               
employees_json
 
=
 
{ID:
 
WorkerDC(ID,
 
username,
 
role,
 
salary)
 
for
 
ID,
 
username,
 
role,
 
salary
 
in
 
employees}
 
               
employees_json
 
=
 
json.dumps(employees_json,
 
default
=
lambda
 
o:
 
o.
__dict__
)

Proccesors
 
|
 
אורי
 
אלפי
 
               
data
 
=
 
"PROCESS_STREAM:"
 
+
 
employees_json
 
               
self
.send_message(data)
 
               
print
(
self
.server.clients)
 
               
time.sleep(
5
)
 
           
except
 
Exception
 
as
 
e
:
 
               
break

(
self
):

connection

.conn.close()

def
 
on_submit
():
 
           
username
 
=
 
username_entry.get().strip()
 
           
password
 
=
 
password_entry.get().strip()
 
           
role
 
=
 
Role.MANAGER
 
if
 
role_var.get()
 
==
 
"Manager"
 
else
 
Role.EMPLOYEE
 
 
           
try
:
 
               
salary
 
=
 
int
(salary_entry.get().strip())
 
           
except
 
ValueError
:
 
               
salary
 
=
 
0
 
 
           
if
 
not
 
username
 
or
 
salary
 
<=
 
0
:
 
               
#
 
You
 
can
 
add
 
a
 
warning
 
here
 
               
return
 
 
           
self
.client.sendall(
f"/add_user
 
{
username
}
 
{
password
}
 
{
role
}
 
{
salary
}\n
"
.encode())
 
 
 
           
#
 
You
 
probably
